The plan is written. Requesting your approval:

**Plan presented for approval:**

### Phase 6: Frontend — campaign list, wizard, live delivery, English and Turkish

#### Context

Phases 1–5 built the API:
- campaign drafts and the segment language;
- the segmentation engine;
- dispatch, and delivery through River with live progress over SSE.

The frontend is still the Phase 1 scaffold: one page with an API status pill. Phase 6 builds
the marketer's interface on the existing Vue 3 + TypeScript + Vite stack. It stays small and
explicit, and its wizard can later open the AI's proposed drafts unchanged (Phase 7).

**What the code already provides**

- **API.**
  - `/api/campaigns`: create, list, get, replace (`PUT`) and delete.
  - `POST /{id}/dispatch`: answers 202, or 409 `already_dispatched`.
  - `GET /api/campaigns/{id}/delivery`: the progress snapshot.
  - `/delivery/events`, the live stream (SSE):
    - each event without a name carries a snapshot;
    - `event: done` carries the final snapshot, and then the stream ends;
    - a `: ping` comment every 15 s;
    - no event IDs.
- **Errors.** Every error answers `{error, problems?}`; each problem is
  `{field, problem, detail?}`. Field paths look like `segment.conditions[2].in[0]`,
  `content.sms.body` and `scheduled_at`.
- **Rules the UI must apply:**
  - **Name:** required, at most 100 characters.
  - **Segment:**
    - 8 condition types, plus nested groups;
    - any condition or group can be negated;
    - at most 3 levels deep and 20 conditions;
    - at most 50 values per list;
    - `within_days` between 1 and 3,650;
    - a `min` or a `max`, neither negative, and `min` ≤ `max`;
    - `total_spent` is in kuruş.
  - **Content:**
    - at least one channel;
    - an SMS of at most 160 characters as written;
    - an email subject of at most 80 characters, and a body;
    - placeholders `{{first_name}}`, `{{last_name}}` and `{{city}}`.
  - **Schedule:** in the future, at most a year ahead.
  - **Saving:** a draft is saved only when complete and valid (decision 19).
- **Frontend.**
  - Vue 3.5, TypeScript 6, Vite 8;
  - Vitest 4 with jsdom and `@vue/test-utils`;
  - oxlint, eslint and prettier, all part of `make check`;
  - nginx already falls back to `index.html` for any path, and doesn't buffer the live
    stream (`X-Accel-Buffering: no`).

**Missing in the backend**

- **No audience count over HTTP.** `segmentation.CountAudience` exists, but decision 22 kept
  the engine off the REST API.
- **No list of what a segment can name:** 3 countries, 28 cities and 8 categories. Names must
  match exactly (`İstanbul`), so free-text inputs would mostly produce `unknown` problems.

#### 1. Backend additions (small and read-only)

`internal/segmentation/http.go` serves two routes, mounted at `/api/segments` in `cmd/api`,
where the service is already built:

| Request | Answer |
|---|---|
| `GET /api/segments/options` | `{countries: [{code, name}], cities: [{name, country}], categories: [name], languages: ["tr", "de", "en"]}` |
| `POST /api/segments/audience`, body `{segment}` | `{matched, reachable, by_channel: {email, sms}}`<br>Errors:<br>• 422 `invalid_segment`, with problems on the same paths as campaigns, unknown names included;<br>• 400 or 413, as elsewhere |

- **Reach.** The estimate always counts both channels, as of the request. The UI then shows
  the reach for the campaign's channels: `reachable` for both, `by_channel[ch]` for one.
- **Counts only.** No customer data is returned. A preview of sample recipients would show
  personal data, so it is deferred.
- **Options.** `Service.Options` in `references.go` runs three ordered queries. A new
  `customer.Languages()` lists the languages, and `Language.Valid` uses it.
- **Handler.** It stays thin: `httpserver.DecodeJSON`, then the service with an injected
  `now`, then `httpserver.WriteJSON`.
- **Stale package docs.**
  - `campaign` still says "nothing schedules or sends them yet".
  - `segmentation` still says "there is no REST API".
- **Tests.** `internal/segmentation/http_test.go`, table-driven with `httptest`, on the
  existing `newShop` fixture:
  - the options lists;
  - known counts;
  - 422 for a structural problem and for an unknown name;
  - 400 for malformed JSON.

#### 2. Frontend structure

**Dependencies.** Add only `vue-router`, at its current stable version. There is no Pinia, UI
library or i18n library, which revises decision 10.

```
src/
  main.ts             app, router, global styles
  App.vue             header (brand, Campaigns, language select, API status), notices, <RouterView>
  router.ts           /campaigns, /campaigns/new, /campaigns/:id, /campaigns/:id/edit; anything else → /campaigns
  api.ts              fetch wrapper throwing ApiError {status, code, problems}; one function per endpoint; JSON types
  campaign.ts         emptyDraft(); condition types and their parameters; placeholders and fill();
                      validateDraft(), mirroring the Go rules with the same field paths and codes
  i18n.ts             locale ref (localStorage, English default), t(key, params), Intl formatting
                      (dates, numbers, TRY, country and language names)
  locales/en.ts, tr.ts  flat dictionaries; tr is typed against en, so a missing key fails type-check
  notify.ts           notice list and notify(), rendered once by App
  styles.css          design tokens and a few shared classes (button, card, badge, field, table)
  views/
    CampaignsView.vue     list
    CampaignEditView.vue  loads a new or existing draft, renders the wizard
    CampaignView.vue      details, draft actions, delivery monitor
  components/
    CampaignWizard.vue    name, three steps, save, send or schedule
    SegmentGroup.vue      recursive group: all/any, negate, its conditions
    ConditionRow.vue      one condition: type, negate, parameters
    AudienceEstimate.vue  debounced count for a segment (steps 1 and 3)
    MessageEditor.vue     SMS and email: toggles, counters, placeholders, previews
    CampaignSummary.vue   read-only draft (review step and details page)
    DeliveryMonitor.vue   live stream and statistics
    ConfirmDialog.vue     native <dialog>; ask() returns a promise
    ApiStatus.vue         existing, translated
```

**Why each component exists:**
- `SegmentGroup` because groups nest;
- `CampaignSummary`, `AudienceEstimate` and `ConfirmDialog` because each is used in two places;
- `DeliveryMonitor` and `MessageEditor` because each has its own lifecycle or logic, tested on
  its own.

There are no composables and no generic form components.

**State** is local to each view and component.
- The wizard owns one reactive `Draft`, copied from its `initial` prop.
- Child components edit their part through `defineModel`: nested fields in place, and a new
  value when a condition changes type.
- The only shared state is the locale ref and the notice list, both plain module values.

#### 3. Pages

- **Campaign list.**
  - **Columns:** a table, stacked cards on phones:
    - the name, linking to the details;
    - a status badge;
    - the channels;
    - the time that matters for the status: updated, scheduled for, started or finished.
  - **Actions:** Edit and Delete, for drafts only; Delete asks first.
  - **States:** a loading placeholder; an empty state with "New campaign"; an error with Retry.
- **Details.**
  - A header with the status and times.
  - Drafts get Edit, "Review and send" (the wizard at step 3) and Delete.
  - A `CampaignSummary`.
  - A dispatched campaign gets a `DeliveryMonitor`, whose snapshots keep the header's status
    current.
  - An unknown ID gets a not-found state.
- **Edit.**
  - A campaign that isn't a draft redirects to its details, with a notice.
  - Leaving with unsaved changes asks first, both when navigating and when closing the tab.

#### 4. Wizard (the Phase 7 seam)

`<CampaignWizard :initial="draft" :campaign-id="id">` never loads a draft itself. So Phase 7
can open an AI-proposed draft in it, problems included, unchanged.

**Flow.**
- **Layout.** The name sits above the steps: Audience → Message → Review.
  - The current step is marked with `aria-current`, and visited steps can be clicked.
  - Next checks the current step's rules; Back is always allowed.
- **Problems.** Client problems (`validateDraft`) and server problems (422) share one shape.
  - Each appears next to its field, matched by path prefix.
  - A failed save jumps to the first step with a problem: `name` and `segment` to 1, `content`
    to 2, `scheduled_at` to 3.
- **Save draft** works on any step once the draft is complete, with POST or PUT. The first save
  replaces the URL with `/campaigns/:id/edit`.
- **Send now and Schedule** (step 3):
  1. a confirmation names the campaign, its channels, its estimated reach and when it will be
     sent;
  2. the draft is saved, then dispatched;
  3. the details page opens, with a notice.

  If dispatch fails after the save (409, or 422 shown in place), the draft stays saved.

**Step 1, Audience.** `SegmentGroup` renders the root (all or any) and the nested groups;
`ConditionRow` renders each condition:
- **Type.** A select with the 8 types; changing it resets the parameters to valid defaults.
- **Exclude** toggles negation, on conditions and groups.
- **Country, city, language and categories** are checkbox lists from `/api/segments/options`:
  - cities are grouped by country;
  - country and language names come from `Intl.DisplayNames`;
  - values missing from the options, as an AI draft could contain, stay visible and are marked
    unknown.
- **Numbers.**
  - `within_days` can be "any time", except for `signed_up`.
  - `min` and `max` count orders, or are entered in TRY for `total_spent` and stored as kuruş
    (× 100).
- **Limits.** Add condition and Add group are disabled at the language's limits (3 levels, 20
  conditions). An empty root reads "All customers".
- **`AudienceEstimate`** sits beside the builder:
  - it waits 400 ms after the last change;
  - it skips segments with client problems, and cancels stale requests;
  - it shows the matched customers and the reach by email and by SMS;
  - unknown names the server reports appear in the builder.

**Step 2, Message (`MessageEditor`).**
- SMS and Email toggles. Turning a channel off keeps its text until the page is left.
- Counters: the SMS body out of 160, and the email subject out of 80. Characters are counted
  the way Go counts them (code points).
- Placeholder chips insert at the cursor.
- Previews fill the placeholders with sample values in the current language: an SMS bubble and
  an email card. They are rendered as text, never as HTML.
- Unknown and malformed placeholders are flagged, as the server does.

**Step 3, Review.**
- `CampaignSummary` shows the segment as a nested list, such as "City: İstanbul, Ankara" or
  "Not purchased · within 30 days", and the messages.
- The estimate for the chosen channels.
- When: Now, or Schedule with a `datetime-local` (in the future, at most a year ahead).
- Save draft, and Send now or Schedule.

#### 5. Delivery monitor (live stream)

- **On mount** it opens `EventSource(/api/campaigns/{id}/delivery/events)`.
  - An event without a name replaces the snapshot.
  - `done` replaces it, closes the source and marks the progress final.
- **Connection:** Live, Reconnecting or Final.
  - While the source is reconnecting by itself (`CONNECTING`), the browser retries.
  - A `CLOSED` source, such as after nginx answers 502 while the API restarts, is reopened by
    the monitor, waiting 1 s and then doubling up to 15 s.
  - On unmount it closes the source and clears its timer.
- **Display.**
  - A status line: scheduled for…, sending, sent and awaiting receipts, completed, or failed.
  - The recipients.
  - One overall progress bar, where processed = total − pending, split into delivered,
    accepted, failed and skipped.
  - An SMS card and an Email card with the five counts. Each status is shown as text and
    colour, with a one-line explanation.
  - `aria-live="polite"` on the summary.

#### 6. Language support

- **Lookup.** `t(key, params)` reads `locale.value`, so templates re-render when the language
  changes. It fills `{name}` parameters and falls back to English.
- **Switcher.** A select in the header: English or Türkçe.
  - The choice is stored in `localStorage`, guarded against unavailable storage.
  - English is the default.
  - `<html lang>` follows the choice.
- **Coverage.** Every user-facing text goes through `t`:
  - labels and statuses;
  - problem codes (`problem.required`, …) and API error codes (`error.already_dispatched`,
    `error.network`, …);
  - dialogs and notices;
  - the sample names in previews.
- **Formatting.** Dates, numbers and TRY use `Intl` in the active language. City and category
  names are shown as stored.

#### 7. UI

- **Style.** Plain CSS:
  - tokens on `:root`: neutral greys, one indigo accent, status colours with AA contrast;
  - the system font stack and an 8 px spacing scale;
  - cards with subtle borders, and visible focus rings;
  - no animation beyond hover and focus transitions.
- **Responsive** at 640 and 960 px: tables become stacked cards, builder rows wrap, and the
  wizard's actions stay at the bottom on phones.
- **Accessible.**
  - A label on every control, and a fieldset with a legend for each checkbox group.
  - `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby` for problems.
  - Focus handled by `<dialog>`, and moved to the step heading when the step changes.

#### 8. Decisions (DECISIONS.md) and what I'm challenging

- **27. A read-only segment API for the composer.** It amends decision 22's "no REST API":
  options and estimates only, counts and never recipients.
- **28. Frontend architecture.** It revises decision 10:
  - Vue Router only, with local state;
  - homegrown i18n and plain CSS;
  - rules mirrored on the client for instant feedback, while the API stays authoritative. Both
    share paths and codes, so their problems render the same way.
  - Dispatch is save then dispatch: two requests, and the draft persists if dispatch fails.
- **Unchanged:** drafts still save only when complete (decision 19). So Save draft points at
  what's missing rather than storing half a draft.

#### 9. Tests (focused)

- **Frontend (Vitest):**
  - **`campaign.spec.ts`:** a `validateDraft` table, one row per rule with its field path;
    and `fill`.
  - **`i18n.spec.ts`:** English by default; switching, persisting and restoring; an invalid
    stored value; the same keys in en and tr.
  - **`api.spec.ts`:** a 422 with problems, a 409 code, and a network failure, each as an
    `ApiError`.
  - **`SegmentGroup.spec.ts`:** add, edit and remove; change type; negate; a nested group;
    limits disabling the buttons; the result equals the expected DSL.
  - **`MessageEditor.spec.ts`:** counters, inserting a placeholder at the cursor, the preview.
  - **`CampaignWizard.spec.ts`:**
    - a new draft through the three steps produces the expected POST body;
    - server 422 problems appear at the right field and step;
    - dispatch happens only after confirming (cancel sends nothing), with save then dispatch
      in order;
    - a schedule in the past is rejected.
  - **`DeliveryMonitor.spec.ts`,** with a fake `EventSource`:
    - the counts per channel;
    - closing on `done`;
    - reopening with backoff after `CLOSED`;
    - closing on unmount.
  - **`CampaignsView.spec.ts`:** loading, empty, error and Retry; delete after confirming.
  - **Existing `App` and `ApiStatus` specs,** updated for the header and the language switch.
- **Go:** `internal/segmentation/http_test.go` (section 1).

#### 10. Docs

- **DECISIONS.md:** entries 27 and 28.
- **README:**
  - status Phase 6, and how to use the app;
  - the two new endpoints;
  - further improvements:

````
## Approved Plan:
# Phase 6: Frontend — campaign list, wizard, live delivery, English and Turkish

## Context

Phases 1–5 built the API:
- campaign drafts and the segment language;
- the segmentation engine;
- dispatch, and delivery through River with live progress over SSE.

The frontend is still the Phase 1 scaffold: one page with an API status pill. Phase 6 builds
the marketer's interface on the existing Vue 3 + TypeScript + Vite stack. It stays small and
explicit, and its wizard can later open the AI's proposed drafts unchanged (Phase 7).

**What the code already provides**

- **API.**
  - `/api/campaigns`: create, list, get, replace (`PUT`) and delete.
  - `POST /{id}/dispatch`: answers 202, or 409 `already_dispatched`.
  - `GET /api/campaigns/{id}/delivery`: the progress snapshot.
  - `/delivery/events`, the live stream (SSE):
    - each event without a name carries a snapshot;
    - `event: done` carries the final snapshot, and then the stream ends;
    - a `: ping` comment every 15 s;
    - no event IDs.
- **Errors.** Every error answers `{error, problems?}`; each problem is
  `{field, problem, detail?}`. Field paths look like `segment.conditions[2].in[0]`,
  `content.sms.body` and `scheduled_at`.
- **Rules the UI must apply:**
  - **Name:** required, at most 100 characters.
  - **Segment:**
    - 8 condition types, plus nested groups;
    - any condition or group can be negated;
    - at most 3 levels deep and 20 conditions;
    - at most 50 values per list;
    - `within_days` between 1 and 3,650;
    - a `min` or a `max`, neither negative, and `min` ≤ `max`;
    - `total_spent` is in kuruş.
  - **Content:**
    - at least one channel;
    - an SMS of at most 160 characters as written;
    - an email subject of at most 80 characters, and a body;
    - placeholders `{{first_name}}`, `{{last_name}}` and `{{city}}`.
  - **Schedule:** in the future, at most a year ahead.
  - **Saving:** a draft is saved only when complete and valid (decision 19).
- **Frontend.**
  - Vue 3.5, TypeScript 6, Vite 8;
  - Vitest 4 with jsdom and `@vue/test-utils`;
  - oxlint, eslint and prettier, all part of `make check`;
  - nginx already falls back to `index.html` for any path, and doesn't buffer the live
    stream (`X-Accel-Buffering: no`).

**Missing in the backend**

- **No audience count over HTTP.** `segmentation.CountAudience` exists, but decision 22 kept
  the engine off the REST API.
- **No list of what a segment can name:** 3 countries, 28 cities and 8 categories. Names must
  match exactly (`İstanbul`), so free-text inputs would mostly produce `unknown` problems.

## 1. Backend additions (small and read-only)

`internal/segmentation/http.go` serves two routes, mounted at `/api/segments` in `cmd/api`,
where the service is already built:

| Request | Answer |
|---|---|
| `GET /api/segments/options` | `{countries: [{code, name}], cities: [{name, country}], categories: [name], languages: ["tr", "de", "en"]}` |
| `POST /api/segments/audience`, body `{segment}` | `{matched, reachable, by_channel: {email, sms}}`<br>Errors:<br>• 422 `invalid_segment`, with problems on the same paths as campaigns, unknown names included;<br>• 400 or 413, as elsewhere |

- **Reach.** The estimate always counts both channels, as of the request. The UI then shows
  the reach for the campaign's channels: `reachable` for both, `by_channel[ch]` for one.
- **Counts only.** No customer data is returned. A preview of sample recipients would show
  personal data, so it is deferred.
- **Options.** `Service.Options` in `references.go` runs three ordered queries. A new
  `customer.Languages()` lists the languages, and `Language.Valid` uses it.
- **Handler.** It stays thin: `httpserver.DecodeJSON`, then the service with an injected
  `now`, then `httpserver.WriteJSON`.
- **Stale package docs.**
  - `campaign` still says "nothing schedules or sends them yet".
  - `segmentation` still says "there is no REST API".
- **Tests.** `internal/segmentation/http_test.go`, table-driven with `httptest`, on the
  existing `newShop` fixture:
  - the options lists;
  - known counts;
  - 422 for a structural problem and for an unknown name;
  - 400 for malformed JSON.

## 2. Frontend structure

**Dependencies.** Add only `vue-router`, at its current stable version. There is no Pinia, UI
library or i18n library, which revises decision 10.

```
src/
  main.ts             app, router, global styles
  App.vue             header (brand, Campaigns, language select, API status), notices, <RouterView>
  router.ts           /campaigns, /campaigns/new, /campaigns/:id, /campaigns/:id/edit; anything else → /campaigns
  api.ts              fetch wrapper throwing ApiError {status, code, problems}; one function per endpoint; JSON types
  campaign.ts         emptyDraft(); condition types and their parameters; placeholders and fill();
                      validateDraft(), mirroring the Go rules with the same field paths and codes
  i18n.ts             locale ref (localStorage, English default), t(key, params), Intl formatting
                      (dates, numbers, TRY, country and language names)
  locales/en.ts, tr.ts  flat dictionaries; tr is typed against en, so a missing key fails type-check
  notify.ts           notice list and notify(), rendered once by App
  styles.css          design tokens and a few shared classes (button, card, badge, field, table)
  views/
    CampaignsView.vue     list
    CampaignEditView.vue  loads a new or existing draft, renders the wizard
    CampaignView.vue      details, draft actions, delivery monitor
  components/
    CampaignWizard.vue    name, three steps, save, send or schedule
    SegmentGroup.vue      recursive group: all/any, negate, its conditions
    ConditionRow.vue      one condition: type, negate, parameters
    AudienceEstimate.vue  debounced count for a segment (steps 1 and 3)
    MessageEditor.vue     SMS and email: toggles, counters, placeholders, previews
    CampaignSummary.vue   read-only draft (review step and details page)
    DeliveryMonitor.vue   live stream and statistics
    ConfirmDialog.vue     native <dialog>; ask() returns a promise
    ApiStatus.vue         existing, translated
```

**Why each component exists:**
- `SegmentGroup` because groups nest;
- `CampaignSummary`, `AudienceEstimate` and `ConfirmDialog` because each is used in two places;
- `DeliveryMonitor` and `MessageEditor` because each has its own lifecycle or logic, tested on
  its own.

There are no composables and no generic form components.

**State** is local to each view and component.
- The wizard owns one reactive `Draft`, copied from its `initial` prop.
- Child components edit their part through `defineModel`: nested fields in place, and a new
  value when a condition changes type.
- The only shared state is the locale ref and the notice list, both plain module values.

## 3. Pages

- **Campaign list.**
  - **Columns:** a table, stacked cards on phones:
    - the name, linking to the details;
    - a status badge;
    - the channels;
    - the time that matters for the status: updated, scheduled for, started or finished.
  - **Actions:** Edit and Delete, for drafts only; Delete asks first.
  - **States:** a loading placeholder; an empty state with "New campaign"; an error with Retry.
- **Details.**
  - A header with the status and times.
  - Drafts get Edit, "Review and send" (the wizard at step 3) and Delete.
  - A `CampaignSummary`.
  - A dispatched campaign gets a `DeliveryMonitor`, whose snapshots keep the header's status
    current.
  - An unknown ID gets a not-found state.
- **Edit.**
  - A campaign that isn't a draft redirects to its details, with a notice.
  - Leaving with unsaved changes asks first, both when navigating and when closing the tab.

## 4. Wizard (the Phase 7 seam)

`<CampaignWizard :initial="draft" :campaign-id="id">` never loads a draft itself. So Phase 7
can open an AI-proposed draft in it, problems included, unchanged.

**Flow.**
- **Layout.** The name sits above the steps: Audience → Message → Review.
  - The current step is marked with `aria-current`, and visited steps can be clicked.
  - Next checks the current step's rules; Back is always allowed.
- **Problems.** Client problems (`validateDraft`) and server problems (422) share one shape.
  - Each appears next to its field, matched by path prefix.
  - A failed save jumps to the first step with a problem: `name` and `segment` to 1, `content`
    to 2, `scheduled_at` to 3.
- **Save draft** works on any step once the draft is complete, with POST or PUT. The first save
  replaces the URL with `/campaigns/:id/edit`.
- **Send now and Schedule** (step 3):
  1. a confirmation names the campaign, its channels, its estimated reach and when it will be
     sent;
  2. the draft is saved, then dispatched;
  3. the details page opens, with a notice.

  If dispatch fails after the save (409, or 422 shown in place), the draft stays saved.

**Step 1, Audience.** `SegmentGroup` renders the root (all or any) and the nested groups;
`ConditionRow` renders each condition:
- **Type.** A select with the 8 types; changing it resets the parameters to valid defaults.
- **Exclude** toggles negation, on conditions and groups.
- **Country, city, language and categories** are checkbox lists from `/api/segments/options`:
  - cities are grouped by country;
  - country and language names come from `Intl.DisplayNames`;
  - values missing from the options, as an AI draft could contain, stay visible and are marked
    unknown.
- **Numbers.**
  - `within_days` can be "any time", except for `signed_up`.
  - `min` and `max` count orders, or are entered in TRY for `total_spent` and stored as kuruş
    (× 100).
- **Limits.** Add condition and Add group are disabled at the language's limits (3 levels, 20
  conditions). An empty root reads "All customers".
- **`AudienceEstimate`** sits beside the builder:
  - it waits 400 ms after the last change;
  - it skips segments with client problems, and cancels stale requests;
  - it shows the matched customers and the reach by email and by SMS;
  - unknown names the server reports appear in the builder.

**Step 2, Message (`MessageEditor`).**
- SMS and Email toggles. Turning a channel off keeps its text until the page is left.
- Counters: the SMS body out of 160, and the email subject out of 80. Characters are counted
  the way Go counts them (code points).
- Placeholder chips insert at the cursor.
- Previews fill the placeholders with sample values in the current language: an SMS bubble and
  an email card. They are rendered as text, never as HTML.
- Unknown and malformed placeholders are flagged, as the server does.

**Step 3, Review.**
- `CampaignSummary` shows the segment as a nested list, such as "City: İstanbul, Ankara" or
  "Not purchased · within 30 days", and the messages.
- The estimate for the chosen channels.
- When: Now, or Schedule with a `datetime-local` (in the future, at most a year ahead).
- Save draft, and Send now or Schedule.

## 5. Delivery monitor (live stream)

- **On mount** it opens `EventSource(/api/campaigns/{id}/delivery/events)`.
  - An event without a name replaces the snapshot.
  - `done` replaces it, closes the source and marks the progress final.
- **Connection:** Live, Reconnecting or Final.
  - While the source is reconnecting by itself (`CONNECTING`), the browser retries.
  - A `CLOSED` source, such as after nginx answers 502 while the API restarts, is reopened by
    the monitor, waiting 1 s and then doubling up to 15 s.
  - On unmount it closes the source and clears its timer.
- **Display.**
  - A status line: scheduled for…, sending, sent and awaiting receipts, completed, or failed.
  - The recipients.
  - One overall progress bar, where processed = total − pending, split into delivered,
    accepted, failed and skipped.
  - An SMS card and an Email card with the five counts. Each status is shown as text and
    colour, with a one-line explanation.
  - `aria-live="polite"` on the summary.

## 6. Language support

- **Lookup.** `t(key, params)` reads `locale.value`, so templates re-render when the language
  changes. It fills `{name}` parameters and falls back to English.
- **Switcher.** A select in the header: English or Türkçe.
  - The choice is stored in `localStorage`, guarded against unavailable storage.
  - English is the default.
  - `<html lang>` follows the choice.
- **Coverage.** Every user-facing text goes through `t`:
  - labels and statuses;
  - problem codes (`problem.required`, …) and API error codes (`error.already_dispatched`,
    `error.network`, …);
  - dialogs and notices;
  - the sample names in previews.
- **Formatting.** Dates, numbers and TRY use `Intl` in the active language. City and category
  names are shown as stored.

## 7. UI

- **Style.** Plain CSS:
  - tokens on `:root`: neutral greys, one indigo accent, status colours with AA contrast;
  - the system font stack and an 8 px spacing scale;
  - cards with subtle borders, and visible focus rings;
  - no animation beyond hover and focus transitions.
- **Responsive** at 640 and 960 px: tables become stacked cards, builder rows wrap, and the
  wizard's actions stay at the bottom on phones.
- **Accessible.**
  - A label on every control, and a fieldset with a legend for each checkbox group.
  - `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby` for problems.
  - Focus handled by `<dialog>`, and moved to the step heading when the step changes.

## 8. Decisions (DECISIONS.md) and what I'm challenging

- **27. A read-only segment API for the composer.** It amends decision 22's "no REST API":
  options and estimates only, counts and never recipients.
- **28. Frontend architecture.** It revises decision 10:
  - Vue Router only, with local state;
  - homegrown i18n and plain CSS;
  - rules mirrored on the client for instant feedback, while the API stays authoritative. Both
    share paths and codes, so their problems render the same way.
  - Dispatch is save then dispatch: two requests, and the draft persists if dispatch fails.
- **Unchanged:** drafts still save only when complete (decision 19). So Save draft points at
  what's missing rather than storing half a draft.

## 9. Tests (focused)

- **Frontend (Vitest):**
  - **`campaign.spec.ts`:** a `validateDraft` table, one row per rule with its field path;
    and `fill`.
  - **`i18n.spec.ts`:** English by default; switching, persisting and restoring; an invalid
    stored value; the same keys in en and tr.
  - **`api.spec.ts`:** a 422 with problems, a 409 code, and a network failure, each as an
    `ApiError`.
  - **`SegmentGroup.spec.ts`:** add, edit and remove; change type; negate; a nested group;
    limits disabling the buttons; the result equals the expected DSL.
  - **`MessageEditor.spec.ts`:** counters, inserting a placeholder at the cursor, the preview.
  - **`CampaignWizard.spec.ts`:**
    - a new draft through the three steps produces the expected POST body;
    - server 422 problems appear at the right field and step;
    - dispatch happens only after confirming (cancel sends nothing), with save then dispatch
      in order;
    - a schedule in the past is rejected.
  - **`DeliveryMonitor.spec.ts`,** with a fake `EventSource`:
    - the counts per channel;
    - closing on `done`;
    - reopening with backoff after `CLOSED`;
    - closing on unmount.
  - **`CampaignsView.spec.ts`:** loading, empty, error and Retry; delete after confirming.
  - **Existing `App` and `ApiStatus` specs,** updated for the header and the language switch.
- **Go:** `internal/segmentation/http_test.go` (section 1).

## 10. Docs

- **DECISIONS.md:** entries 27 and 28.
- **README:**
  - status Phase 6, and how to use the app;
  - the two new endpoints;
  - further improvements:
    - a preview of sample recipients;
    - browser end-to-end tests;

    - a hint for multi-part SMS;
````

```
// Languages returns the supported languages.
func Languages() []Language {
	return []Language{LanguageTurkish, LanguageGerman, LanguageEnglish}
}

// Valid reports whether l is a supported language.
func (l Language) Valid() bool { return slices.Contains(Languages(), l) }
```

I'll make `fill` follow Go's `Fill` exactly, and truncate problem details at 40 characters as Go does, so the same problem reported by both client and server compares equal:

**Edit** `<repo>/frontend/src/campaign.ts`

```
// fill replaces each placeholder in text with its value, as delivery does for each recipient
// (campaign.Fill). Braces that aren't a placeholder are kept as written.
export function fill(text: string, values: Record<(typeof placeholders)[number], string>): string {
  let out = ''
  for (;;) {
    const open = text.indexOf('{{')
    const end = open < 0 ? -1 : text.indexOf('}}', open + 2)
    if (end < 0) break
    const name = text.slice(open + 2, end).trim()
    out += text.slice(0, open) + (isPlaceholder(name) ? values[name] : text.slice(open, end + 2))
    text = text.slice(end + 2)
  }
… [22 more characters]
```

Vitest supports `test.execArgv`. Turning Node's storage off at the root makes jsdom's `localStorage` visible by itself and silences the warning, so the setup override can go:

**Edit** `<repo>/frontend/vitest.config.ts`

```
      environment: 'jsdom',
      // Node's own localStorage (unusable without a file) would hide jsdom's.
      execArgv: ['--no-experimental-webstorage'],
      setupFiles: ['src/__tests__/setup.ts'],
```

Only the two browser-test campaigns are there, with all their jobs completed. Deleting them, their deliveries, the mock provider's rows and the finished jobs in one transaction:

**Bash** — Delete the browser-test campaigns and their records

```sh
cd "<repo>" && docker compose exec -T postgres sh -c 'psql -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$POSTGRES_DB" -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -q' <<'SQL'
BEGIN;
DELETE FROM mock_provider_messages
 WHERE idempotency_key IN (SELECT 'delivery-' || id FROM deliveries WHERE campaign_id IN (10, 11));
DELETE FROM deliveries WHERE campaign_id IN (10, 11);
DELETE FROM river_job WHERE state = 'completed'
   AND kind IN ('dispatch_campaign', 'send_message', 'delivery_receipt');
DELETE FROM campaigns WHERE id IN (10, 11);
COMMIT;
SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM campaigns) AS campaigns, (SELECT count(*) FROM deliveries) AS deliveries,
       (SELECT count(*) FROM mock_provider_messages) AS mock, (SELECT count(*) FROM river_job) AS jobs,
       (SELECT count(*) FROM customers) AS customers;
SQL
```

↳ result

```
campaigns | deliveries | mock | jobs | customers 
-----------+------------+------+------+-----------
         0 |          0 |    0 |    0 |      5000
```

```
# Campaign Composer

An AI-powered marketing campaign composer. A marketer describes a campaign in natural
language; an LLM proposes audience segmentation rules and SMS/email content; people review,
edit and approve it; approved campaigns are sent asynchronously to synthetic users through
mock providers, and delivery status is tracked in real time.

> **Status — Phase 5 (delivery).** On top of the Phase 1 foundation (PostgreSQL,
> migrations, health probes, structured logs, graceful shutdown, and a web app showing the
> API's readiness), the customer and commerce model holds 5,000 synthetic customers and their
> shopping activity. Marketers manage campaign drafts through the REST API, and the
> segmentation engine turns a draft's segment into the customers it reaches on each channel.
… [5,434 more characters]
```

```
**Consequences.** Frontend and backend build independently. Server-sent events through nginx
will need `proxy_buffering off` on their location.

*Revised by decision 28:* only Vue Router was added; state stays local and the styling is
plain CSS. Decision 26 settled the event streams with `X-Accel-Buffering: no`.
```

```
  use.
```

```
## 27. A read-only segment API for the composer

**Context.** While a segment is edited, the composer must show whom it reaches. It must also
offer the countries, cities and categories a segment can name: names match exactly
(`İstanbul`), so free text would mostly fail as `unknown`. Decision 22 kept segmentation off
the REST API.

**Decision.** `internal/segmentation` serves two read-only routes at `/api/segments`
(`http.go`), amending decision 22:
```

```
285:- [ ] **Segment-aware SMS limits.** Count GSM-7 and UCS-2 message segments instead of 160
286-      characters: Turkish letters force UCS-2, which fits 70 characters per message.
287-- [ ] **Optimistic locking for campaign drafts.** Concurrent edits are last-write-wins today.
288-- [ ] **Dispatch exactly what was reviewed.** Dispatch could take the version the reviewer
--
291:- [ ] **Unschedule and cancel.** The lifecycle allows scheduled → draft, but no endpoint does it
292-      yet, and a dispatch in progress can't be stopped.
293-- [ ] **Receipt timeout.** A real provider can lose a receipt, which would leave its message
294-      `accepted` and the progress never final.
--
312:- [ ] **Pagination for the campaign list.**
313:- [ ] **A shared error framework** with stable codes, translations and HTTP mapping at the API
… [80 more characters]
```

```
- [ ] **Pagination, filters and search for the campaign list,** in the API and the web app.
- [ ] **A shared error framework** with stable codes, translations and HTTP mapping at the API
      boundary. Error bodies are local to each capability's handlers today.
- [ ] **A preview of sample recipients** in the composer. It would show personal data, so it
      needs a decision on who may see it; the estimate shows counts only today (§27).
- [ ] **Browser end-to-end tests** in CI (e.g. Playwright). Component tests run in jsdom
      today, and the main flows were checked in Chrome by hand (PHASE_REVIEWS.md, Phase 6).
- [ ] **Live statuses in the campaign list.** It shows them as they were when it loaded;
      only a campaign's own page follows them live.
- [ ] **Dark mode.** The colours are tokens on `:root`, so a dark set could be added in one
      place.
```

Draft rules mirror `internal/campaign` with the same field paths and codes; when one changes, change both.

```
Browser checks of the web app (http://localhost:3000 from the Compose stack): there is no `chromium-cli` and no Playwright browser download; `playwright-core` installed into a scratchpad folder (`npm install --prefix <dir> --ignore-scripts playwright-core`) drives the installed Chrome headless via `executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'`. Load it from a script in another folder with `createRequire(<dir>/package.json)`. Full-page screenshots draw the sticky header mid-page; that's an artifact, not a bug. The Phase 6 run (2026-11-15) found a real bug this way.

**Why:** the Node and `.env` gotchas each cost a failed verification run on 2026-11-14.
```

Phase 6 is built and checked, and nothing is committed yet.

#### What's new
- **Two read-only API routes** in `internal/segmentation`: `GET /api/segments/options` (the countries, cities, categories and languages a segment can name) and `POST /api/segments/audience` (how many customers match and how many each channel reaches). They return counts only, never customers. This reverses the earlier decision to keep segmentation off the REST API; DECISIONS.md §27 records why.
- **Frontend** (the only new dependency is `vue-router`):
  - **Campaign list:** status, channels and dates; drafts can be edited or deleted after a confirmation.
  - **Three-step composer:**
    1. **Audience:** a visual builder covering every condition type, AND/OR groups, nesting and exclusion, with a live estimate.
    2. **Messages:** SMS and email, with counters, placeholders inserted at the cursor, and previews.
    3. **Review:** save, send now, or schedule, each after a confirmation.
  - **Campaign page:** live delivery with SMS and email shown separately, five statuses, and automatic reconnection.
  - **Languages:** English and Turkish, with the choice remembered after a reload.
- **Phase 7:** the wizard is handed a draft and never loads one itself, so an AI-proposed draft can open in it unchanged.

#### Verification
- **Tests:** `make check` passes (79 frontend tests, Go lint 0 issues), and so do the Go tests against PostgreSQL, including 10 new cases for the segment API.
- **Mutation check:** I broke six behaviours one at a time, and the matching test failed each time.
- **Chrome, through nginx:** two scripted runs, with no console errors:
  - A campaign estimated to reach 538 customers was sent to exactly 538 recipients.
  - A campaign scheduled from the review step started 11 ms after its time.
  - Restarting the API mid-stream: the live view showed "Reconnecting…", then went live again.
  - Turkish stayed selected after a reload.
  - Delete and keyboard-only use both worked.
  - The phone layout has no horizontal scrolling.
- **Cleanup:** I deleted the test campaigns afterwards; 5,000 customers remain.
- **Screenshots** are in `/private/tmp/claude-502/<project>/61d394fd-a454-4187-b6af-7b54c26af34f/scratchpad/screens/`.

#### Fixed along the way
- **A bug the browser run caught:** reopening a saved draft locked steps 2 and 3, so "Review and send" couldn't jump to the review step. All steps of a saved draft now open, and a test covers it.
- **Number fields update when you leave the field,** not on every keystroke. Otherwise typing "10.05" lira gets rewritten to "10" partway.
- **Test setup:** the test runner now disables Node 26's own `localStorage`, which hid the test browser's. A setup file adds the dialog methods the test browser lacks.

#### Worth knowing
- **The draft rules exist twice,** in Go and in `frontend/src/campaign.ts`, for instant feedback. The API stays the authority: if the two drift apart, the save still shows the API's problem at the right field. CLAUDE.md now says to change both together.
- **Vue Router 5 pulls in 33 extra packages for a build plugin we don't use.** None of them end up in the shipped app. Version 4.6 would add only one.
- **Each open campaign page keeps a live stream,** and each stream reads the database every second.
